   166	---
   167	
   168	# AGMSG-TASK dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01 — INV-6 reset of a01 on tools/worker-write-boundary-a01, seat a010; plan 024 P1-15 under D-024-17, D-024-19, D-024-20, D-024-21 and D-024-23
   169	
   170	## Goal
   171	
   172	This is the amendment-1 text of the task dispatched at 2026-10-19T05:42Z (the ninth-round text that passed its pre-dispatch security review). Amendment 1 folds in two interpretation notes the worker needed (the inherited make format clause; the portable tracked `.claude/settings.json` with host-specific paths only in the rendered guard) and removes the Codex worker profile item (a Codex seat edits no Codex-boundary source; it becomes the separate Claude-seat task dotfiles-T133-codex-worker-network-a01). Everything else is the dispatched text in meaning; the branch, the eight green tests and the fixtures stay; the worker swaps the task copy to this text at its next push.
   173	
   174	The host is not a security boundary between seats sharing one OS user. gh keys Keychain credentials by host and user, so a worker running as the same macOS user can read the orchestrator's token regardless of `GH_CONFIG_DIR`. Prevention of a worker-initiated merge requires a separate OS user or host under D-024-23's future P1-16, which the operator decides whether to pursue. Until then the GitHub boundary is detection, not prevention. The regime detects missing acceptance or gate-passage evidence in the next records PR, not at main-push time, because the records reach main only with that boundary PR. On a PR touching `.orchestration/acceptance/**`, the regime job checks every main merge commit since the previous boundary for its corresponding acceptance record with gate-passage evidence and fails the PR when one is missing. P1-11 owns implementation of that check; this task states section 5.14 item 0's detection rule. Approvals and gate evidence of record are read from GitHub at a SHA through its API inside the sandbox. Host-local controls reduce accidental and injected damage without establishing isolation from a malicious same-UID process.
   175	
   176	This task implements section 5.14 items 1, 9 and 10 through a dedicated Claude worker configuration directory, static Edit deny rules on trusted paths and worktree configuration, managed sandbox enforcement, guarded project hooks, hook interpreter and PATH bindings, private worker caches and the rule that worker commands run inside their sandbox. Every Claude seat has `sandbox.failIfUnavailable: true`.
   177	
   178	The administrative managed policy is shared by every Claude seat. Its Herdr socket allowance and three control-plane exclusions remain available to workers as well as the orchestrator. Empty arrays in worker user settings do not subtract these managed entries. Their availability is a residual under item 0, and the worker execution rule supplies no authorization to use them outside the sandbox.
   179	
   180	[memory:decision] D-024-20 makes this task the dispatch prerequisite of P1-14 a05, P1-10 a02, audit-pool-a02 and P1-6. The orchestrator places this task in their `after:` fields before dispatch. This task has `after: []`. A Codex seat performs these Claude configuration edits under the routing rule, including the Worker Playbook step 4 sentence permitted by plan 021 step 8.3. This Codex seat edits no Codex-boundary source.
   181	
   182	The design source is `.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md` section 5.14 items 0 through 10. D-024-21, D-024-23 and the amended items 0, 1 and 8 govern this revision. D-024-22's proposed gh credential separation is superseded. The acceptance record preserves documentation facts and earlier proposals as history.
   183	
   184	The third C-80 text review is `.orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-ecc7ab5.md.last.md`. Its five P1 findings remain recorded. The retained mechanisms are documented symlink-aware deny rules, approval reads outside the local object database, hook interpreter bindings and runtime hook failure policy.
   185	
   186	The fourth C-80 text review is `.orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-8d42532.md.last.md`. Its six P1 findings remain recorded. The retained mechanisms are the static manifest deny inventory, protection of the orchestrator worklog and cache, narrow home denies around writable agmsg stores, correct linked-worktree hook placement and fixed hook-runtime PATH.
   187	
   188	The fifth C-80 text review is `.orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-08e6ac7.md.last.md`. Its three P1 findings remain recorded. This revision removes the gh credential-separation mechanism, records the upstream agmsg store-driver residual and moves sandbox enforcement into administrative managed settings.
   189	
   190	The file-tool rule is the documented sentence, “Deny rules: apply when either the requested path or the file it resolves to matches.” Edit and Write refuse a requested file that is itself a symlink. Directory symlinks are subject to resolved-target permission checks, and the tools check the resolved location again when opening the approved file. These documented checks reduce redirection risk without establishing an atomic host security boundary against concurrent same-UID changes. [Claude permission reference](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permissions#symlinks)
   191	
   192	The 2026-10-19T03:45:06Z sandbox probe ran `gh api repos/mryfmo/dotfiles --jq .name` and returned `dotfiles`. The recorded sandbox proxy used CONNECT without TLS interception, and gh read the Keychain token. This historical observation confirms neither credential separation nor final-head deployment readiness.
   193	
   194	The documentation facts recorded at 03:2xZ establish that Edit denies also affect the Bash sandbox, hooks reload during a session and an unavailable sandbox is fail-open by default. D-024-21's 04:0xZ decision establishes the dedicated Claude configuration mechanism, directory-specific Claude credentials, symlink coverage and `onFailure: "block"` support. These Claude credentials do not separate gh Keychain access.
   195	
   196	The settings documentation states, “In a worktree, it uses the file at the main checkout’s root.” Linked-worktree sessions therefore load the main checkout's `.claude/settings.local.json`. Worker hooks belong in `~/.claude-worker/settings.json`, and the main checkout's local hooks also receive the orchestrator-only guard. [Claude local settings reference](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings#where-claude-code-keeps-the-local-file-in-a-git-repository)
   197	
   198	## Files
   199	
   200	1. `home/dot_agents/agent-config.yaml` remains the source of agent policy and model profiles.
   201	
   202	   The manifest names `orchestrator_checkout` as the anchored primary checkout and supplies trusted deployment bindings for the guard, hook scripts and absolute interpreters. These bindings resolve outside worker worktrees and executable caches. Worker cwd, tool input, worktree configuration and inherited command variables do not select them. The manifest also supplies the trusted bindings from which the render resolves the absolute directories holding `ruff`, `prettier` and `terminal-browser`, without using mise shims.
   203	
   204	   The manifest defines a separate Claude worker settings output at `home/.chezmoitemplates/claude-worker-settings.json` and an administrative managed-settings output at `home/.chezmoitemplates/claude-managed-settings.json`. The worker configuration directory is `~/.claude-worker`. The launcher passes its expanded absolute path as `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`.
   205	
   206	   A static `trusted_paths:` list names every protected top-level path of the anchored checkout and the protected home paths. It explicitly includes `<anchored-checkout>/.agents/worklog/**` and `<anchored-checkout>/.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**`. The `.claude` entries protect tracked contents and the main checkout's `.claude/settings.local.json` without denying the whole `.claude/worktrees/` tree. The list explicitly names directory targets and descendant patterns where both are required.
   207	
   208	   Separate worker Edit denies protect `//<anchored-checkout>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**` and `//<anchored-checkout>/.claude/worktrees/*/.mcp.json`. The `.claude` directory target is also protected. Ordinary worktree source files and the per-seat cache remain outside these configuration denies.
   209	
   210	   The home-path denies are `~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`, `~/.agents/model-profiles.env`, `~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`, `~/.agents/skills/**/SKILL.md`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins`, `~/.claude/**`, `~/.claude-worker/**`, `~/.local/bin/**`, `~/.local/share/**`, `~/.local/state/**`, `~/.local/share/mise/**`, `~/.config/**`, `~/.cache/uv/**` and `~/.local/share/uv/**`. No blanket deny on `~/.agents/**` or `~/.local/**` replaces this list.

   166	---
   167	
   168	# AGMSG-TASK dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01 — INV-6 reset of a01 on tools/worker-write-boundary-a01, seat a010; plan 024 P1-15 under D-024-17, D-024-19, D-024-20, D-024-21 and D-024-23
   169	
   170	## Goal
   171	
   172	This is the amendment-1 text of the task dispatched at 2026-10-19T05:42Z (the ninth-round text that passed its pre-dispatch security review). Amendment 1 folds in two interpretation notes the worker needed (the inherited make format clause; the portable tracked `.claude/settings.json` with host-specific paths only in the rendered guard) and removes the Codex worker profile item (a Codex seat edits no Codex-boundary source; it becomes the separate Claude-seat task dotfiles-T133-codex-worker-network-a01). Everything else is the dispatched text in meaning; the branch, the eight green tests and the fixtures stay; the worker swaps the task copy to this text at its next push.
   173	
   174	The host is not a security boundary between seats sharing one OS user. gh keys Keychain credentials by host and user, so a worker running as the same macOS user can read the orchestrator's token regardless of `GH_CONFIG_DIR`. Prevention of a worker-initiated merge requires a separate OS user or host under D-024-23's future P1-16, which the operator decides whether to pursue. Until then the GitHub boundary is detection, not prevention. The regime detects missing acceptance or gate-passage evidence in the next records PR, not at main-push time, because the records reach main only with that boundary PR. On a PR touching `.orchestration/acceptance/**`, the regime job checks every main merge commit since the previous boundary for its corresponding acceptance record with gate-passage evidence and fails the PR when one is missing. P1-11 owns implementation of that check; this task states section 5.14 item 0's detection rule. Approvals and gate evidence of record are read from GitHub at a SHA through its API inside the sandbox. Host-local controls reduce accidental and injected damage without establishing isolation from a malicious same-UID process.
   175	
   176	This task implements section 5.14 items 1, 9 and 10 through a dedicated Claude worker configuration directory, static Edit deny rules on trusted paths and worktree configuration, managed sandbox enforcement, guarded project hooks, hook interpreter and PATH bindings, private worker caches and the rule that worker commands run inside their sandbox. Every Claude seat has `sandbox.failIfUnavailable: true`.
   177	
   178	The administrative managed policy is shared by every Claude seat. Its Herdr socket allowance and three control-plane exclusions remain available to workers as well as the orchestrator. Empty arrays in worker user settings do not subtract these managed entries. Their availability is a residual under item 0, and the worker execution rule supplies no authorization to use them outside the sandbox.
   179	
   180	[memory:decision] D-024-20 makes this task the dispatch prerequisite of P1-14 a05, P1-10 a02, audit-pool-a02 and P1-6. The orchestrator places this task in their `after:` fields before dispatch. This task has `after: []`. A Codex seat performs these Claude configuration edits under the routing rule, including the Worker Playbook step 4 sentence permitted by plan 021 step 8.3. This Codex seat edits no Codex-boundary source.

 succeeded in 121ms:
   181	
   182	The design source is `.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md` section 5.14 items 0 through 10. D-024-21, D-024-23 and the amended items 0, 1 and 8 govern this revision. D-024-22's proposed gh credential separation is superseded. The acceptance record preserves documentation facts and earlier proposals as history.
   183	
   184	The third C-80 text review is `.orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-ecc7ab5.md.last.md`. Its five P1 findings remain recorded. The retained mechanisms are documented symlink-aware deny rules, approval reads outside the local object database, hook interpreter bindings and runtime hook failure policy.
   185	
   186	The fourth C-80 text review is `.orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-8d42532.md.last.md`. Its six P1 findings remain recorded. The retained mechanisms are the static manifest deny inventory, protection of the orchestrator worklog and cache, narrow home denies around writable agmsg stores, correct linked-worktree hook placement and fixed hook-runtime PATH.
   187	
   188	The fifth C-80 text review is `.orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-08e6ac7.md.last.md`. Its three P1 findings remain recorded. This revision removes the gh credential-separation mechanism, records the upstream agmsg store-driver residual and moves sandbox enforcement into administrative managed settings.
   189	
   190	The file-tool rule is the documented sentence, “Deny rules: apply when either the requested path or the file it resolves to matches.” Edit and Write refuse a requested file that is itself a symlink. Directory symlinks are subject to resolved-target permission checks, and the tools check the resolved location again when opening the approved file. These documented checks reduce redirection risk without establishing an atomic host security boundary against concurrent same-UID changes. [Claude permission reference](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permissions#symlinks)
   191	
   192	The 2026-10-19T03:45:06Z sandbox probe ran `gh api repos/mryfmo/dotfiles --jq .name` and returned `dotfiles`. The recorded sandbox proxy used CONNECT without TLS interception, and gh read the Keychain token. This historical observation confirms neither credential separation nor final-head deployment readiness.
   193	
   194	The documentation facts recorded at 03:2xZ establish that Edit denies also affect the Bash sandbox, hooks reload during a session and an unavailable sandbox is fail-open by default. D-024-21's 04:0xZ decision establishes the dedicated Claude configuration mechanism, directory-specific Claude credentials, symlink coverage and `onFailure: "block"` support. These Claude credentials do not separate gh Keychain access.
   195	
   196	The settings documentation states, “In a worktree, it uses the file at the main checkout’s root.” Linked-worktree sessions therefore load the main checkout's `.claude/settings.local.json`. Worker hooks belong in `~/.claude-worker/settings.json`, and the main checkout's local hooks also receive the orchestrator-only guard. [Claude local settings reference](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings#where-claude-code-keeps-the-local-file-in-a-git-repository)
   197	
   198	## Files
   199	
   200	1. `home/dot_agents/agent-config.yaml` remains the source of agent policy and model profiles.
   201	
   202	   The manifest names `orchestrator_checkout` as the anchored primary checkout and supplies trusted deployment bindings for the guard, hook scripts and absolute interpreters. These bindings resolve outside worker worktrees and executable caches. Worker cwd, tool input, worktree configuration and inherited command variables do not select them. The manifest also supplies the trusted bindings from which the render resolves the absolute directories holding `ruff`, `prettier` and `terminal-browser`, without using mise shims.
   203	
   204	   The manifest defines a separate Claude worker settings output at `home/.chezmoitemplates/claude-worker-settings.json` and an administrative managed-settings output at `home/.chezmoitemplates/claude-managed-settings.json`. The worker configuration directory is `~/.claude-worker`. The launcher passes its expanded absolute path as `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`.
   205	
   206	   A static `trusted_paths:` list names every protected top-level path of the anchored checkout and the protected home paths. It explicitly includes `<anchored-checkout>/.agents/worklog/**` and `<anchored-checkout>/.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**`. The `.claude` entries protect tracked contents and the main checkout's `.claude/settings.local.json` without denying the whole `.claude/worktrees/` tree. The list explicitly names directory targets and descendant patterns where both are required.
   207	
   208	   Separate worker Edit denies protect `//<anchored-checkout>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**` and `//<anchored-checkout>/.claude/worktrees/*/.mcp.json`. The `.claude` directory target is also protected. Ordinary worktree source files and the per-seat cache remain outside these configuration denies.
   209	
   210	   The home-path denies are `~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`, `~/.agents/model-profiles.env`, `~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`, `~/.agents/skills/**/SKILL.md`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins`, `~/.claude/**`, `~/.claude-worker/**`, `~/.local/bin/**`, `~/.local/share/**`, `~/.local/state/**`, `~/.local/share/mise/**`, `~/.config/**`, `~/.cache/uv/**` and `~/.local/share/uv/**`. No blanket deny on `~/.agents/**` or `~/.local/**` replaces this list.
   211	
   212	   The agmsg store paths `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/teams`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/run` and `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/ext-tools` remain writable. Deny patterns cover neither these directory targets nor their descendants except the file `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins`. Edit denies merge into the sandbox configuration, so Bash writes to that file and `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` are refused too. Their influence on installed delivery-script driver and plugin selection remains the separately owned agmsg residual.
   213	
   214	   The orchestrator retains its existing permissions, cache allowances and hook functions within the administrative managed policy. Its user baseline receives `failIfUnavailable: true`, `onFailure: "block"` on its hooks and the interpreter and PATH bindings below. 71:- A task that touches a trust boundary (anything the design tier lists, CI that decides a required check, code that executes PR-controlled input) carries a `threat_table` in its front matter: one `{control, disposition}` row per thing the PR or the orchestrator controls (the tree and its tests, task.md, workflow files, interpreter and environment files, in-process effects, new or renamed scripts, metadata separators). The row unit is one row per `allowed_files` entry, one row per listed control that is not a path (task metadata, workflow and interpreter or environment inputs, in-process effects, new or renamed scripts, metadata separators), carried by the declared test module's row where that is where the control acts, and one row per orchestrator effect from the fixed list (forced removal, merge, update-branch); under a wildcard `allowed_files` entry whose files are executed or applied (scripts, workflows, hooks, settings, the rules seats follow), one row per concrete file the PR changes; a wildcard over test fixtures or other data files keeps one row; C4 and C7b of `scripts/orchestrator/task-consistency-check.py` verify the path half before dispatch, and the task-file review checks the rest. A disposition takes one of three forms: `closed: tests/<file>::<name>`, the test that closes the row; `residual: <sentence>`, the compensating control; `out-of-scope: <task id>`, the task that owns the row. The task-file review checks the three shape rules by hand: a `closed` selector names a test present in the PR tree at acceptance, an `out-of-scope` owner is a task id in the design's `implementing_tasks`, a `residual` payload is a sentence of at least 20 characters (the gate's minimum for a reason). For a `closed` row it checks the selector's form only, since the test does not exist before implementation. Substance is the reviewers': the task-file review, the Bot and the auditor judge each row against the diff. The table is the task's completion condition: a missing row is added once, counted as an amendment, never fixed finding by finding.

   166	---
   167	
   168	# AGMSG-TASK dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01 — INV-6 reset of a01 on tools/worker-write-boundary-a01, seat a010; plan 024 P1-15 under D-024-17, D-024-19, D-024-20, D-024-21 and D-024-23
   169	
   170	## Goal
   171	
   172	This is the amendment-1 text of the task dispatched at 2026-10-19T05:42Z (the ninth-round text that passed its pre-dispatch security review). Amendment 1 folds in two interpretation notes the worker needed (the inherited make format clause; the portable tracked `.claude/settings.json` with host-specific paths only in the rendered guard) and removes the Codex worker profile item (a Codex seat edits no Codex-boundary source; it becomes the separate Claude-seat task dotfiles-T133-codex-worker-network-a01). Everything else is the dispatched text in meaning; the branch, the eight green tests and the fixtures stay; the worker swaps the task copy to this text at its next push.
   173	
   174	The host is not a security boundary between seats sharing one OS user. gh keys Keychain credentials by host and user, so a worker running as the same macOS user can read the orchestrator's token regardless of `GH_CONFIG_DIR`. Prevention of a worker-initiated merge requires a separate OS user or host under D-024-23's future P1-16, which the operator decides whether to pursue. Until then the GitHub boundary is detection, not prevention. The regime detects missing acceptance or gate-passage evidence in the next records PR, not at main-push time, because the records reach main only with that boundary PR. On a PR touching `.orchestration/acceptance/**`, the regime job checks every main merge commit since the previous boundary for its corresponding acceptance record with gate-passage evidence and fails the PR when one is missing. P1-11 owns implementation of that check; this task states section 5.14 item 0's detection rule. Approvals and gate evidence of record are read from GitHub at a SHA through its API inside the sandbox. Host-local controls reduce accidental and injected damage without establishing isolation from a malicious same-UID process.
   175	
   176	This task implements section 5.14 items 1, 9 and 10 through a dedicated Claude worker configuration directory, static Edit deny rules on trusted paths and worktree configuration, managed sandbox enforcement, guarded project hooks, hook interpreter and PATH bindings, private worker caches and the rule that worker commands run inside their sandbox. Every Claude seat has `sandbox.failIfUnavailable: true`.
   177	
   178	The administrative managed policy is shared by every Claude seat. Its Herdr socket allowance and three control-plane exclusions remain available to workers as well as the orchestrator. Empty arrays in worker user settings do not subtract these managed entries. Their availability is a residual under item 0, and the worker execution rule supplies no authorization to use them outside the sandbox.
   179	
   180	[memory:decision] D-024-20 makes this task the dispatch prerequisite of P1-14 a05, P1-10 a02, audit-pool-a02 and P1-6. The orchestrator places this task in their `after:` fields before dispatch. This task has `after: []`. A Codex seat performs these Claude configuration edits under the routing rule, including the Worker Playbook step 4 sentence permitted by plan 021 step 8.3. This Codex seat edits no Codex-boundary source.
   181	
   182	The design source is `.orchestration/plans/024-design-quality.md` section 5.14 items 0 through 10. D-024-21, D-024-23 and the amended items 0, 1 and 8 govern this revision. D-024-22's proposed gh credential separation is superseded. The acceptance record preserves documentation facts and earlier proposals as history.
   183	
   184	The third C-80 text review is `.orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-ecc7ab5.md.last.md`. Its five P1 findings remain recorded. The retained mechanisms are documented symlink-aware deny rules, approval reads outside the local object database, hook interpreter bindings and runtime hook failure policy.
   185	
   186	The fourth C-80 text review is `.orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-8d42532.md.last.md`. Its six P1 findings remain recorded. The retained mechanisms are the static manifest deny inventory, protection of the orchestrator worklog and cache, narrow home denies around writable agmsg stores, correct linked-worktree hook placement and fixed hook-runtime PATH.
   187	
   188	The fifth C-80 text review is `.orchestration/validation/dotfiles-T133-worker-write-boundary-a01-text-security-08e6ac7.md.last.md`. Its three P1 findings remain recorded. This revision removes the gh credential-separation mechanism, records the upstream agmsg store-driver residual and moves sandbox enforcement into administrative managed settings.
   189	
   190	The file-tool rule is the documented sentence, “Deny rules: apply when either the requested path or the file it resolves to matches.” Edit and Write refuse a requested file that is itself a symlink. Directory symlinks are subject to resolved-target permission checks, and the tools check the resolved location again when opening the approved file. These documented checks reduce redirection risk without establishing an atomic host security boundary against concurrent same-UID changes. [Claude permission reference](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/permissions#symlinks)
   191	
   192	The 2026-10-19T03:45:06Z sandbox probe ran `gh api repos/mryfmo/dotfiles --jq .name` and returned `dotfiles`. The recorded sandbox proxy used CONNECT without TLS interception, and gh read the Keychain token. This historical observation confirms neither credential separation nor final-head deployment readiness.
   193	
   194	The documentation facts recorded at 03:2xZ establish that Edit denies also affect the Bash sandbox, hooks reload during a session and an unavailable sandbox is fail-open by default. D-024-21's 04:0xZ decision establishes the dedicated Claude configuration mechanism, directory-specific Claude credentials, symlink coverage and `onFailure: "block"` support. These Claude credentials do not separate gh Keychain access.
   195	
   196	The settings documentation states, “In a worktree, it uses the file at the main checkout’s root.” Linked-worktree sessions therefore load the main checkout's `.claude/settings.local.json`. Worker hooks belong in `~/.claude-worker/settings.json`, and the main checkout's local hooks also receive the orchestrator-only guard. [Claude local settings reference](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/settings#where-claude-code-keeps-the-local-file-in-a-git-repository)
   197	
   198	## Files
   199	
   200	1. `home/dot_agents/agent-config.yaml` remains the source of agent policy and model profiles.
   201	
   202	   The manifest names `orchestrator_checkout` as the anchored primary checkout and supplies trusted deployment bindings for the guard, hook scripts and absolute interpreters. These bindings resolve outside worker worktrees and executable caches. Worker cwd, tool input, worktree configuration and inherited command variables do not select them. The manifest also supplies the trusted bindings from which the render resolves the absolute directories holding `ruff`, `prettier` and `terminal-browser`, without using mise shims.
   203	
   204	   The manifest defines a separate Claude worker settings output at `home/.chezmoitemplates/claude-worker-settings.json` and an administrative managed-settings output at `home/.chezmoitemplates/claude-managed-settings.json`. The worker configuration directory is `~/.claude-worker`. The launcher passes its expanded absolute path as `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`.
   205	
   206	   A static `trusted_paths:` list names every protected top-level path of the anchored checkout and the protected home paths. It explicitly includes `<anchored-checkout>/.agents/worklog/**` and `<anchored-checkout>/.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**`. The `.claude` entries protect tracked contents and the main checkout's `.claude/settings.local.json` without denying the whole `.claude/worktrees/` tree. The list explicitly names directory targets and descendant patterns where both are required.
   207	
   208	   Separate worker Edit denies protect `//<anchored-checkout>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**` and `//<anchored-checkout>/.claude/worktrees/*/.mcp.json`. The `.claude` directory target is also protected. Ordinary worktree source files and the per-seat cache remain outside these configuration denies.
   209	
   210	   The home-path denies are `~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`, `~/.agents/model-profiles.env`, `~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`, `~/.agents/skills/**/SKILL.md`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins`, `~/.claude/**`, `~/.claude-worker/**`, `~/.local/bin/**`, `~/.local/share/**`, `~/.local/state/**`, `~/.local/share/mise/**`, `~/.config/**`, `~/.cache/uv/**` and `~/.local/share/uv/**`. No blanket deny on `~/.agents/**` or `~/.local/**` replaces this list.
   211	
   212	   The agmsg store paths `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/teams`, `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/run` and `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/ext-tools` remain writable. Deny patterns cover neither these directory targets nor their descendants except the file `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins`. Edit denies merge into the sandbox configuration, so Bash writes to that file and `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` are refused too. Their influence on installed delivery-script driver and plugin selection remains the separately owned agmsg residual.
   213	
   214	   The orchestrator retains its existing permissions, cache allowances and hook functions within the administrative managed policy. Its user baseline receives `failIfUnavailable: true`, `onFailure: "block"` on its hooks and the interpreter and PATH bindings below. Its exclusion entries match the three control-plane entries specified below; the former Linux `gh *` exclusion is removed.
   215	
   216	   The worker user policy contains no excluded commands and sets `allowUnsandboxedCommands: false`. It contains no Herdr socket allowance and no allow-all Unix socket switch. Its sandbox is enabled and has `failIfUnavailable: true`. Shared managed entries remain effective despite these empty worker arrays.
   217	
   218	   Worker `sandbox.filesystem.allowWrite` contains only the existing agmsg store paths and per-seat cache paths. The worktree is the project root and supplies its ordinary project write root. Shared uv and mise write allowances are not copied into worker settings.
   219	
   220	   Existing model selections, Codex worker profile values, Codex sandbox and approval arguments and Codex shared Git grants retain their meaning and remain unchanged. The deterministic Codex permgate rule that stops approving sandbox escapes is an operator step.
   221	
   222	2. `scripts/generate-agent-configs.py` renders the distinct orchestrator user, worker user and administrative managed baselines from the manifest.
   223	
   224	   The trusted-path deny inventory is rendered from the static `trusted_paths:` list. Rendering performs no Git object traversal, `git ls-tree` enumeration or tracked-path discovery. Worker-writable refs, replacement refs and objects do not determine the list.
   225	
   226	   Anchored checkout rules use absolute `//` permission patterns. Rendering includes the explicitly listed trusted paths themselves and their descendants. It emits no checkout-wide parent deny that defeats ordinary worktree writes. The separate worktree-configuration denies remain present.
   227	
   228	   Edit denies cover Write, MultiEdit and NotebookEdit through Claude's permission system. Requested-path and resolved-target matching supply the documented symlink coverage. The generator creates no `executable_worker-write-boundary.py` or replacement PreToolUse path validator.
   229	
   230	   Every direct Git verification introduced or changed by this task follows section 5.14 item 2. It begins with `env -i`, uses the selected absolute Git executable and an explicit `-C`, sets `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1` and `GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null`, and supplies `-c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always`. Diff and show also use `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` and `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false`.
   231	
   232	   The private executable-link directory contains only links to selected absolute trusted binaries. Rendering rejects missing bindings, worker-controlled executable paths, shims, empty PATH entries and relative PATH entries. Python invocations use `-I`. Hook execution uses the role-specific pinned PATH bindings specified below. The render test asserts that `ruff`, `prettier` and `terminal-browser` resolve under the rendered orchestrator tool PATH on the rendering host and that no entry of that PATH is a shims directory.
   233	
   234	   `tests/unit/test_generate_agent_configs.py::GenerateAgentConfigsTest::test_worker_deny_inventory_is_static_and_matches_manifest` asserts that the rendered list equals the manifest entry after trusted anchor and home expansion. It explicitly asserts the rendered home-path deny patterns `~/.local/share/**` and `~/.local/state/**`, including the application code under `~/.local/share` used by `~/.local/bin` wrappers such as `terminal-browser`. It reads the filesystem of the test's own checkout and asserts that every tracked top-level source path is represented. It invokes no Git command to discover those paths.
   235	
   236	   The inventory fixtures cover files, directories, `.claude` children, the main-local settings file, ordinary worktree writes, both worktree-configuration patterns, an anchor containing spaces and both explicit worklog patterns. Adding a top-level source path without a manifest entry makes the coverage assertion fail. Planted Git environment and replacement-ref fixtures leave rendering unchanged.
   237	
   238	   `tests/unit/test_claude_worker_settings.py::ClaudeWorkerSettingsTest::test_worker_denies_leave_agmsg_stores_writable` asserts the rendered deny patterns `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` and `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` and verifies that the four store directories `db`, `teams`, `run` and `ext-tools` and their descendant fixtures remain writable except that one file.
   239	
   240	3. `home/.chezmoitemplates/claude-settings-managed.json` remains the orchestrator user baseline.

- design conclusion (03:5xZ, pending two fact checks): three text rounds show the host is not a security boundary between seats that share one OS user; the regime's hard boundary is GitHub (protected main, required checks, the merge by the orchestrator's identity), and inputs that must resist a malicious worker (the approval ledger, the gate's evidence of record) are read from GitHub at a sha through the API inside the sandbox, never from local files or the local object database; host-local controls target accidental and injected damage and are stated as such. The per-seat mechanism that makes worker-specific policy possible at all is a separate `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` for worker seats (own settings: no excluded commands, no Herdr socket, minimal trusted hooks with absolute interpreters and `onFailure: block`, Edit denies on trusted paths, failIfUnavailable true); the facts (credentials with a second config dir; onFailure; sandbox per config dir; symlink resolution of file-tool paths) are being verified against the documentation.

99:## 5.14 信頼ホスト実行契約（trusted-host execution contract、D-024-18）
103:1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。
104:2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`: privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE` before the first statement, and the first statement re-executes the program under `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME="$HOME" LANG=C.UTF-8 <SENTINEL>=1 /bin/bash -p --noprofile --norc "$0" "$@"` unless its sentinel is set; sanitization performed by later statements (unset -f, compgen, PATH edits) is not a boundary, because the statements themselves resolve through the untrusted names (PR #410 security reviews 513e5d0 and 6b7e783, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
109:0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
111:9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。
113:7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Format files reported by Claude Code hook JSON input.

The hook reads the complete JSON event from stdin, extracts every edited file path
from common Write/Edit/MultiEdit payload shapes, filters by suffix, and runs the
formatter for that suffix without going through a shell. ruff and prettier come
from PATH: their versions are pinned in the mise config, and ruff.toml and
.prettierignore keep vendored and record paths untouched.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import shlex
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

PYTHON_COMMANDS = [
    ["ruff", "format"],
]
MARKDOWN_COMMANDS = [
    ["prettier", "--write"],
]


def collect_paths(value: Any) -> set[Path]:
    paths: set[Path] = set()
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        for key, item in value.items():
            if key in {"file_path", "path"} and isinstance(item, str):
                paths.add(Path(item))
            else:
                paths.update(collect_paths(item))
    elif isinstance(value, list):
        for item in value:
            paths.update(collect_paths(item))
    return paths


def repository_root(path: Path) -> Path:
    """The git work tree containing path, else its directory: where the formatter configs live."""
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(
            ["git", "-C", str(path.parent), "rev-parse", "--show-toplevel"], capture_output=True, text=True, check=False
        )
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return path.parent
    root = result.stdout.rstrip("\n")
    return Path(root) if result.returncode == 0 and root else path.parent


def run_commands(commands: list[list[str]], files: list[Path]) -> int:
    status = 0
    # Run from each file's repository root: prettier reads .prettierignore from
    # its working directory, and the session's directory may be another tree.
    by_root: dict[Path, list[str]] = {}
    for path in files:
        resolved = path.resolve()
        by_root.setdefault(repository_root(resolved), []).append(str(resolved))
    for root, file_args in sorted(by_root.items()):
        for command in commands:
            config = root / "ruff.toml"
            if command[0] == "ruff" and config.is_file():
                # Pin the root config: ruff otherwise picks each file's nearest
                # pyproject.toml (vendor/compactiondb) and skips the root exclusions.
                command = [*command, "--config", str(config)]
            try:
                result = subprocess.run(command + file_args, cwd=root, check=False)
            except FileNotFoundError:
                # make update installs every declared mise tool, so a missing formatter means that step was skipped or failed.
                print(
                    f"{command[0]} is not installed; run `make update` (it installs every declared mise tool)",
                    file=sys.stderr,
                )
                status = max(status, 1)
                continue
            status = max(status, result.returncode)
    return status


def main() -> int:
    raw = sys.stdin.read()
    if not raw.strip():
        return 0
    try:
        payload = json.loads(raw)
    except json.JSONDecodeError as error:
        print(f"failed to parse Claude hook input: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 0

    paths = sorted(path for path in collect_paths(payload.get("tool_input", payload)) if path.exists())
    python_files = [path for path in paths if path.suffix == ".py"]
    markdown_files = [path for path in paths if path.suffix == ".md"]

    status = 0
    status = max(status, run_commands(PYTHON_COMMANDS, python_files))
    status = max(status, run_commands(MARKDOWN_COMMANDS, markdown_files))
    return status


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
#!/bin/bash

# @file home/dot_claude/hooks/executable_project-map-reload.sh
# @brief Reload the visible project map after a Claude file edit.
# @description
#   Pass CDP on both calls to avoid terminal-browser issue 60.
#   Pin tab selection and guard reloads against navigation away from a project map.
#   Match the event file path before choosing an existing map tab.
#   Canonicalize event paths and file URLs with the installed isolated Python stdlib.
#   Reject encoded URL separators before decoding them into filesystem paths.
#   Bind the reload guard to the canonical-matched URL, encoded as a JSON string.
#   Open a separate right split using half the width when the parsed listing yields no map tab.
#   shortcut: rejected map metadata or racing writes can open another pane; tighten selection in the hook follow-up.
# @exitcode 0 Always, including unavailable tools or browsers.

# @description Return the physical absolute path of a local path or file URL.
# @arg $1 path Event path or file URL, including home and relative forms.
# @stdout Canonical path.
canonical_path() {
    python3 -I -S -c '
import os
import sys
from urllib.parse import unquote, urlsplit

path = sys.argv[1]
if path.startswith("file://"):
    url = urlsplit(path)
    if url.netloc not in ("", "localhost") or "%2f" in url.path.lower():
        sys.exit(1)
    path = unquote(url.path, errors="strict")
if path == "~" or path.startswith("~/"):
    path = os.path.expanduser("~") + path[1:]
print(os.path.realpath(path))
' "$1" 2>/dev/null
}

file_path=$(jq -r '.tool_input.file_path // empty' 2>/dev/null) || exit 0
[[ -n "$file_path" ]] || exit 0
event_path=$(canonical_path "$file_path") || exit 0
[[ "$event_path" == */.project-map/index.html ]] || exit 0

listing=$(terminal-browser ls --all --json 2>/dev/null) || exit 0
candidates=$(jq -c '
    [.browsers[]? | .cdpPort as $port
     | select($port | type == "number")
     | select($port >= 1 and $port <= 65535 and $port == ($port | floor))
     | .tabs[]?
     | select(.url | strings | startswith("file://"))
     | select(.targetId | strings | test("^[A-Fa-f0-9]{32}\\z"))
     | [$port, .targetId, .url]][]
' <<< "$listing" 2>/dev/null) || exit 0
selection=""
while IFS= read -r candidate; do
    [[ -n "$candidate" ]] || continue
    tab_path=$(canonical_path "$(jq -r '.[2]' <<< "$candidate")") || continue
    [[ "$tab_path" == "$event_path" ]] || continue
    selection=$(jq -r '.[0:2] | @tsv' <<< "$candidate")
    selected_url=$(jq -c '.[2]' <<< "$candidate")
    break
done <<< "$candidates"
if [[ -z "$selection" ]]; then
    terminal-browser open "$file_path" --split right --size 0.5 --no-merge >/dev/null 2>&1
    exit 0
fi
IFS=$'\t' read -r port target_id <<< "$selection"

agent="${TERMINAL_BROWSER_AGENT:-$HOME/.local/share/terminal-browser/app/agent-browser/bin/agent-browser}"
"$agent" --cdp "$port" --session project-map-reload --pin-tab tab "$target_id" >/dev/null 2>&1 || exit 0
"$agent" --cdp "$port" --session project-map-reload --pin-tab eval "if (location.href === new URL($selected_url).href) location.reload()" >/dev/null 2>&1
exit 0

## Completion

The existing branch `tools/permission-guard-gate-a01` continues PR #420 from `911f5202e61a2e46f527fcd9a4eda0041e97410a`. The first push replaces the a04 branch task copy with the byte-identical dispatched a05 copy. Seat a010 keeps its branch, fixes and evidence. The reset creates no second implementation PR. The missed `2026-10-19T03:00Z` deadline remains reported, and the orchestrator owns the new estimate.

The Completion clause of `.orchestration/tasks/dotfiles-T129-normalised-comparison-lint-a01.md` applies with this task's existing branch, the qualifications below, `dotfiles-T133-permission-guard-gate-followup-a01` as the owner of any advisory P2, and `task_copy=<sha256 of .orchestration/dotfiles-T133-permission-guard-gate-a05/task.md>` in RESULT. The orchestrator writes the owner task before assigning its first advisory P2. The six reset defects and retained duplicate-rule defect belong to this task.

Every worker-side fix round, before or after the first RESULT, counts toward INV-6 under C-61. Predecessor counters, the a03 Bot correction, its single CI-repair round, question PONGs, S2 withdrawal and a04 S1 stop remain historical records. They are not recast as approvals.

The worker's own pre-push review is one round at most. A surviving P0/P1 is sent in RESULT as open and stops integration. The worker starts no further private correction-and-review cycle to avoid reporting it. Permitted later rounds remain subject to INV-6 and the inherited completion rule.

No `@codex review` comment is posted. Automatic final-head reviews are observed within the bounded 15-minute window, and absence is recorded as `bot: none`. Required GitHub Actions checks pass before RESULT. Every feedback item carries its proposed disposition. Surviving P0/P1 findings remain open for the operator's decision.

A Codex seat writes evidence at the same relative paths in its worktree and says so in RESULT. Worker review evidence uses the required `-worker-` infix. Agent-side review evidence and its receipt follow the repository's Crit-data rules without opening a browser review or asking the user to review.

RESULT names the final head, base, branch, changed files, Bot findings and dispositions, every open P0/P1, exact test selectors, measured caps, all six new deployable scripts, the orch-gate.sh change, task-copy SHA256 and evidence paths with the SHA256 values consumed by the entrypoint. The report distinguishes predecessor observations from final-head closure and records the indirect-command-recognition residual, interpreter-launch premise, Claude-seat path-deny gap, same-UID process residual and unmitigated outside-sandbox gh exclusion owned by P1-15 under D-024-17. The orchestrator copies worktree evidence by absolute path before acceptance review.

This task is `security: true`. Under C-80, a security-profile context reviews this complete a05 task text before dispatch. That review is a dispatch prerequisite rather than a claim made by this draft. The orchestrator also runs the task-level audit and a separate security-profile review on the final implementation head.

The finding-class table includes `decision-not-bound-to-execution` and preserves the predecessor classes `argv-text-mismatch`, `unbound-evidence` and `fail-open-on-error`. A third non-advisory finding of one class stops the artifact for redesign. It never starts another instance-by-instance correction round. The acceptance record retains the required class search and re-plan evidence.

Final-head feedback collection, task-level audit, finding dispositions, acceptance and the integration gate follow Orchestrator Playbook step 10 in its prescribed order. The gate receives `PR_FEEDBACK_EVIDENCE`, `AUDIT_EVIDENCE`, `ACCEPTANCE_EVIDENCE` and the agent review receipt. A changed head receives fresh required evidence. The security review of that final head precedes the gate.

The deployed hook allows only the anchored tracked `integrate-merge.sh` invocation through updated input naming the verified realpath. The script itself runs each gh and git write as a separate command, without shell chaining. Callers submit the entrypoint as one command with its three arguments. The eventual merge is exactly `gh pr merge --squash --match-head-commit <audited head> <pr>`.

Until PR #420 lands, strings carrying `gh pr merge` or `gh pr create` are written with the file-edit tool rather than Bash heredocs or echo. This includes test fixtures, reports and validation prose. The worker does not deploy the new hook or relax its tracked-main identity conditions to bootstrap this PR.

Verdict: incorrect## 5.14 信頼ホスト実行契約（trusted-host execution contract、D-024-18）

orchestrator 側の仕組み（merge gate、integrate-merge、orch-gate、dispositions、audit-pool、verdict、chain）は、同じホスト上で同じ UID の worker プロセスと共存する。2026-10-19 の C-80 審査（a05、dispositions-a02、audit-pool-a01 の 3 文、計 19 件の P1）は、どの文も同じ前提を書かず同じ穴を持つことを示した。以後、`security: true` の orchestrator 側タスク文は本節を参照で適用し、本節の各項目に対応する threat 行を持つ。文ごとに言い換えない。

1. **書き込み境界（前提 6、ランタイム別に正直に）** Claude worker 席は専用の設定ディレクトリ `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=~/.claude-worker`（chezmoi が描画、operator が一度 login）で起動し、orchestrator の `~/.claude` と設定を共有しない。worker 設定：除外コマンド無し、Herdr socket 無し、sandbox の allowWrite は自席 worktree と agmsg store と席別キャッシュだけ、`failIfUnavailable: true`、信頼パス（anchored checkout の `.claude/worktrees/` 以外、`~/.agents`、`~/.claude*`、`~/.local`、`~/.config`）への Edit deny（公式文書：file tool の permission 検査は要求パスと symlink 解決後のパスの両方に deny を適用し、symlink そのものへの書き込みは拒否する）、sandbox の強制範囲はユーザー設定ではなく **managed settings**（`/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、公式文書 "Repository settings under an admin-required sandbox"：project/local 設定は sandbox を無効化したり除外・socket を足したりできない）に置く。chezmoi は user ではこのファイルを書けないので、描画した内容を操作者が `sudo` で一度配置する（操作者手順）。worker 設定は worktree 自身の `.claude/**` と `.mcp.json` への Edit deny（`//<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/*/.claude/**`）を持つ（Bash 側は sandbox 既定の settings/hooks の denyWithinAllow）。agmsg store（全席が書く）から配送 hook が driver や plugin を source する上流の経路（storage.sh の `drivers.partition` traversal、`db/trusted-plugins` と `plugins/partition/shared.sh` による差し替え承認）は別タスク（agmsg hardening）の行で、worker 設定は `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/plugins/**` と `~/.agents/skills/agmsg/db/trusted-plugins` への書き込みを deny し（Edit deny は sandbox の deny にも合流する）、hook 側は store 由来の設定値を検証するまで残余と書く。managed settings の配置先は OS で異なる（macOS `/Library/Application Support/ClaudeCode/managed-settings.json`、Linux `/etc/claude-code/managed-settings.json`）。hook は agmsg 配送だけを、hook コマンドを `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME=<home> …` で始めて（`BASH_ENV` などの継承環境を bash 起動前に消す；PATH 固定だけでは BASH_ENV が先に走る）絶対パスのインタプリタで呼び（上流の agmsg スクリプトは `/usr/bin/env bash` の shebang を持つので、hook 実行環境の PATH が shim を含まないことで束縛する）、各 hook に `onFailure: "block"`。公式文書：linked worktree の session は **main checkout の `.claude/settings.local.json` を読む**ので、worker 向けの hook を worktree の settings.local.json に書いても読まれず、逆に orchestrator の local hooks が worker session でも走る。したがって orchestrator 側の local hooks も guard 経由にし、worker 側の hook は worker config dir の settings に置く。Edit deny の一覧は `git ls-tree` ではなく描画時の静的一覧（manifest に列挙、`.agents/worklog/**` と `.agents/worklog/uv-cache/**` を含む）とし、`~/.agents/**` の全面 deny は agmsg store の書き込みと矛盾するので、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.agents/skills/**/scripts/**`、`~/.agents/model-profiles.env` などストア以外を列挙する。公式文書で確認した事実（2026-10-19）：`Edit(path)` の deny は Write/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit にも効き、deny → ask → allow の順で最初の一致が決まる；ただし Read/Edit の deny は **Bash sandbox の設定にも合流する**（"Paths and domains from both sandbox settings and permission rules are merged into the final sandbox configuration"）ので、ユーザー設定（全席共有）に `.orchestration/**` の Edit deny を書くと orchestrator の Bash による記録書き込みも止まる。hook は起動時に固定されず、設定ファイルの編集は file watcher が即座に反映する。`sandbox.failIfUnavailable` の既定は fail-open（依存が無ければ sandbox 無しで実行）。したがって Claude 席の境界は Edit deny 規則ではなく、**ユーザーレベルの PreToolUse hook**（`~/.claude/hooks/worker-write-boundary`、chezmoi が描画、`/bin/sh` または絶対パスの `python3 -I`）で作る：cwd の realpath が `<anchored>/.claude/worktrees/` 配下の session では、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit の対象が cwd の外、または `<cwd>/.claude/**`・`<cwd>/.mcp.json` のとき `permissionDecision: deny` を返す。Bash 側は sandbox の既定 `denyWithinAllow`（settings、hooks）と `allowWrite`（自席 worktree）が担う。`failIfUnavailable: true` を全席に置く。 Codex 席は sandbox（workspace-write）がツールの書き込み全部を自席の worktree と列挙パスに限る。Claude 席は Seatbelt sandbox が Bash を限るだけで、Write/Edit/MultiEdit/NotebookEdit、hook、MCP プロセスは sandbox 外で動き、permission policy（permgate の決定論ポリシー）が path で拒否しない限り anchored checkout や `~/.agents` にも書ける（2026-10-19 時点、描画済み設定にその deny は無い）。したがって前提 6 は「Codex 席は成立、Claude 席は P1-15 が描画設定に Edit の path deny を入れるまで残余」と書く。P1-15 以前に「worker は信頼ルートに書けない」と書く文は誤りで、C-80 が拒否する。P1-15 が入るまで、承認の出所は項目 8 の「コミット済み台帳」で守る。書き込みは自席の worktree（`.claude/worktrees/<name>`）、scratch、列挙されたツールキャッシュに限る。anchored checkout（`.claude/worktrees/` 以外）、`~/.agents/agent-config.yaml`、`~/.claude/hooks`、信頼ルートのバイナリ、orchestrator の証跡・スナップショット・lock は worker から書けない。render テストが描画後の deny を検証する。残余は「どの席 sandbox にも属さない同一 UID プロセス」と、Worker Playbook step 4 が sandbox 外に出す少数のコマンド（gh）。後者は D-024-17（P1-15）で絶対パスの実行体に束縛する。
2. **無害化した環境を最初に** 最初の処理として `env -i` に入り、PATH は信頼ルートで解決した絶対バイナリへのリンクだけの私設ディレクトリ（shims、空、相対は拒否）。git は毎回 `GIT_CONFIG_NOSYSTEM=1 GIT_CONFIG_GLOBAL=/dev/null -c core.hooksPath=/dev/null -c core.fsmonitor=false -c protocol.allow=never -c protocol.https.allow=always` と明示 `-C`（https だけ許す。`protocol.allow=never` 単独は必要な fetch を止める）。 検査の読み取りは `GIT_NO_LAZY_FETCH=1` で部分クローンの遅延 fetch（checkout 設定の credential.helper を起動しうる）を止め、`-c submodule.recurse=false --ignore-submodules=all` で submodule 内の status/diff（submodule 自身の設定が filter を有効化しうる）を止める。`.gitmodules` を持つ checkout は検査対象として拒否する。 `-C` は作業木を束縛しないので（repo-local の `core.worktree` が読み先を checkout 外へ向けられる）、git は `--git-dir=<checkout>/.git --work-tree=<checkout>` を明示し、`git rev-parse --show-toplevel` の realpath が checkout と一致しないときは拒否する。 premise や検査の「コマンド実行」は、固定語彙の読み取り専用プローブ（`git rev-parse <ref>`、`test -e <path>`、`grep -c <pattern> <file>` のように検査器自身が解釈する形）だけを許し、任意のシェル文字列は実行しない（本文のハッシュはコマンド文を認証するだけで、`python -I scripts/x.py` のファイル被演算子や `source ./x.sh` の中身は認証しない）。diff と show は `--no-ext-diff --no-textconv` と `-c core.attributesFile=/dev/null -c core.sshCommand=false` を付け、PR 側の `.gitattributes`（external diff、textconv、filter）が検査内容や実行物を変えないようにする。Python は `-I`。PATH 探索で実行するものは無い。 A shell script that runs under a PR-controlled or worker-controlled environment (verdict.sh, the chain glue, CI steps) is executed as a program with `#!/bin/bash -p`, never as `bash <file>`: privileged mode discards inherited functions, `BASH_ENV`/`ENV`, `SHELLOPTS`, `BASHOPTS`, `CDPATH` and `GLOBIGNORE` before the first statement, and the first statement re-executes the program under `/usr/bin/env -i PATH=/usr/bin:/bin HOME="$HOME" LANG=C.UTF-8 <SENTINEL>=1 /bin/bash -p --noprofile --norc "$0" "$@"` unless its sentinel is set; sanitization performed by later statements (unset -f, compgen, PATH edits) is not a boundary, because the statements themselves resolve through the untrusted names (PR #410 security reviews 513e5d0 and 6b7e783, 2026-10-19). No script accepts a caller-supplied executable directory; interpreters are the fixed absolute paths item 9 names.
3. **信頼パスの境界検査** 仕組みが信頼するパス（スクリプト、台帳、証跡、worktree、lock、出力先）は realpath で解決し、`.claude/worktrees/` または manifest の worker ルート配下なら拒否（ask/hold）。symlink 構成要素は解決後に比較し、大小文字を区別しないファイルシステムでは同一性比較を大小文字非依存で行う。
4. **検証したものを実行し、検証した内容を渡す** 検証した実行体はその realpath で実行する（再探索しない）。検証したファイル内容は信頼ルート配下のスナップショット（0600、一意名）として渡し、使用直前に sha256 を再照合する。判断に読んだ証跡は sha256 を出力に記録する。
5. **出力と予約の同一性** 名前には 40 桁の head を使う（7 桁で衝突させない）。lock は信頼ルート配下の固定 inode に O_EXCL で作り、置換を inode 比較で検出する。物理スロット 1 つに lock 1 つ。
6. **正直な残余** 境界だけが緩和するリスクを `closed` と書かない。各文は残余を 1 行で明記する。
0. **脅威モデル（D-024-21）** 席は同じ OS ユーザで動き、ホストは席間の信頼境界ではない（同一 UID のプロセスは symlink 差し替え、共有 `.git` の object 差し替え、hook の子プロセス、Herdr socket 経由の host 実行で、ホスト内のどの検査も迂回できる）。regime の硬い境界は GitHub（保護された main、必須チェック、orchestrator の identity によるマージ）。ただしこの境界は **worker 席が orchestrator の GitHub 資格情報を共有しない**ときだけ成立する（D-024-22：worker 席は専用の GitHub identity（別アカウントまたは App のトークン）で `gh` に login し、ruleset の bypass 一覧に入らない。orchestrator の keychain トークンを worker の config dir に持ち込まない。identity が分かれるまで「GitHub が境界」は未成立と書く）。**同一 macOS ユーザでは資格情報は分離できない**（gh は keychain の項目を host/user で引き、同じ UID の gh は orchestrator のトークンを読める。`GH_CONFIG_DIR` は設定を分けるだけ）。防止には worker 席を別の OS ユーザ（または別ホスト）で動かすしかなく、それは D-024-23 の将来項目（P1-16、操作者の判断）。それまで GitHub 境界は **検知**で守る：記録は boundary の記録 PR まで main に無いので、検知は次の記録 PR の regime ジョブで行う（`.orchestration/acceptance/**` に触れる PR で、前回 boundary 以降の main の各マージ commit に、gate 通過の証跡を持つ受け入れ記録が対応することを検査し、無ければその PR を失敗させる；P1-11 owner a02 の行 `ci:detection:1`）。main push 時点の検知は記録が無いので不可能であり、文は「次の記録 PR で検知、防止ではない」と書く。悪意ある worker に耐えるべき入力（承認台帳、gate が読む証跡 of record）は GitHub から sha 指定で API 経由（sandbox 内）に読み、ローカルファイルやローカル object database からは読まない。ホスト内の制御（本節 1〜10）は事故と prompt injection による損害の抑制であり、文はそう書く。
8. **承認はコミット済み台帳から読む** permission の決定行（`scope=permission approved_by=user diff_sha256=…`）は anchored checkout の作業コピーではなく、コミット済みの台帳から読む。読み方は `git ls-remote origin refs/heads/main` が返す commit sha を取り、GitHub API（`gh api repos/<owner>/<repo>/contents/.orchestration/decisions.tsv?ref=<sha>`、sandbox 内）でその sha の内容を取得する。ローカルの `git show` は共有 object database が書けるため中間 tree の差し替えで偽物を返しうる（第 3 回審査の所見 2）（ローカルの ref 名 `origin/main` は経由しない。`refs/replace/` も書けるので replacement を無効にする。Codex worker は `.git/objects` と `.git/refs` に書けるので ref は差し替えられるが、sha で指した object は内容アドレスで偽造できない）。作業コピーの行は提案で、記録 PR が main にマージされて初めて承認になる（PR の承認は操作者の操作）。偽造には main へのマージが要る。
9. **私設キャッシュ** orchestrator 側の実行は worker と共有するキャッシュ（`~/.cache/uv`、`~/.local/share/uv`、mise）から実行可能物を読まない。`UV_CACHE_DIR` を anchored checkout 配下（`.agents/worklog/uv-cache`、gitignored、worker 書き込み不可）に固定し、`uv run --no-config --no-project --offline --python <信頼ルートの絶対パスのインタプリタ>` で起動する（`--no-config` で uv の設定探索を止め、`--python` の絶対パスで cwd や親の `.venv` の発見を止める；敵対的な `.venv` と `uv.toml` を置いた fixture で検証）。hook は uv を使わず jq と python 標準ライブラリだけで動く。
10. **worker は sandbox 外でコマンドを実行しない（D-024-17 の改訂）** 2026-10-19T03:45Z の実測で `gh api` は Claude sandbox の中から成功した（sandbox の proxy は CONNECT 型で TLS を終端しない；keychain のトークンも読めた）。worker 席の除外コマンドは空にし、gh も git push も sandbox 内の proxy 経由で実行する。Codex 席は `sandbox_workspace_write.network_access = true` で同じ状態にし、permgate の codex ポリシーは sandbox 外への escape を承認しない（permgate の変更は操作者の手順）。wrapper 方式（絶対パス、env -i、固定 GH_CONFIG_DIR）は LD_PRELOAD と引数転送（`gh repo clone -- --config`）を閉じられないので採らない。
7. **検証** threat_table は本節の項目 1〜10 に 1 行ずつ対応させる（参照だけでは足りない）。共通テスト補助 `tests/unit/trusted_exec_contract.py`（P1-6 で作る）を使い、仕組みごとに 4 つの red→green テストを持つ：PATH 先頭の shim が実行されない、植え付けた GIT_* と git 設定が効かない、worker ルート配下の信頼パスが拒否される、検証後に差し替えたファイルが消費側に届かない。
